     }\n1490\t    }\n1491\t}\n1492\t\n1493\t/// Map of config field paths to their source.\n1494\t///\n1495\t/// Used by `needle config --dump --show-source` to annotate each value.\n1496\tpub type ConfigLoader {\n1648\t    /// Load global config from `~/.config/needle/config.yaml`.\n1649\t    ///\n1650\t    /// If the file does not exist, returns the default config (not an error).\n1651\t    pub fn load_global() -> Result<Config> {\n1652\t        let path = dirs_or_home(\".config/needle/config.yaml\");\n1653\t        Self::load_from_path(&path)\n1654\t    }\n1655\t\n1656\t    /// Load config from a specific path.\n1657\t    ///\n1658\t    /// If the file does not exist, returns the default config.\n1659\t    pub fn load_from_path(path: &Path) -> Result<Config> {\n1660\t        if !path.exists() {\n1661\t            return Ok(Config::default());\n1662\t        }\n1663\t        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path)\n1664\t            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to read config file: {}\", path.display()))?;\n1665\t        let config: Config = serde_yaml::from_str(&text)\n1666\t            .with_context(|| format!(\"invalid YAML in config file: {}\", path.display()))?;\n1667\t        Ok(config)\n1668\t    }\n1669\t\n1670\t    /// Load workspace overrides from `.needle.yaml` in the given workspace root.\n1671\t    ///\n1672\t    /// Returns `None` if the file does not exist. Emits warnings for\n1673\t    /// non-overridable keys found in the workspace config.\n1674\t    pub fn load_workspace(workspace_root: &Path) -> Result<Option<WorkspaceOverrides>> {\n1675\t        let          .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to read workspace config: {}\", path.display()))?;\n1681\t\n1682\t        // Check for non-overridable keys and warn.\n1683\t        Self::warn_non_overridable_keys(&text, &path)?;\n1684\t\n1685\t        let overrides: WorkspaceOverrides = serde_yaml::from_str(&text)\n1686\t            .with_context(|| format!(\"invalid YAML in workspace config: {}\", path.display()))?;\n1687\t        Ok(Some(overrides))\n1688\t    }\n1689\t\n1690\t    /// Warn about non-overridable top-level keys in workspace config YAML.\n1691\t    fn warn_non_overridable_keys(yaml_text: &str, path: &Path) -> Result<()> {\n1692\t        let value: serde_yaml::Value = serde_yaml::from_str(yaml_text)\n1693\t            .with_context(|| format!(\"invalid YAML in workspace config: {}\", path.display()))?;\n1694\t\n1695\t        if let serde_yaml::Value::Mapping(map) = value {\n1696\t            for key in map.keys() {\n1697\t                if let serde_yaml::Value::String(key_str) = key {\n1698\t                    if NON_OVERRIDABLE_KEYS.contains(&key_str.as_str()) {\n1699\t                        tracing::warn!(\n1700\t                            key = %key_str,\n1701\t                            path = %path.display(),\n1702\t                            \"workspace config contains non-overridable setting '{}' — ignored\",\n1703\t                            key_str,\n1704\t                        );\n1705\t                    }\n1706\t                }\n1707\t            }\n1708\t        }\n1709\t        Ok(())\n1710\t    }\n1711\t\n1712\t    /// Apply workspace overrides to a config.\n1713\t    ///\n1714\t    /// Only overridable fields are applied. Records sources in the source map.\n1715\t    pub fn apply_workspace(\n1716\t        config: &mut Config,\n1717\t        overrides: &WorkspaceOverrides,\n1718\t        ws_path: &Path,\n1719\t        sources: &mut SourceMap,\n1720\t    ) {\n1721\t        let source = ConfigSource::WorkspaceFile(ws_path.join(\".needle.yaml\"));\n1722\t\n1723\t        if let Some(ref agent) = overrides.agent {\n1724\t            if let Some(ref default) = agent.default {\n1725\t                config.agent.default = default.clone();\n1726\t                sources.insert(\"agent.default\".to_string(), source.clone());\n1727\t            }\n1728\t            if let Some(timeout) = agent.timeout {\n1729\t                config.agent.timeout = timeout;\n1730\t                sources.insert(\"agent.timeout\".to_string(), source.clone());\n1731\t            }\n1732\t            if let Some(ref routing) = agent.routing {\n1733\t                config.agent.routing = Some(routing.clone());\n1734\t                sources.insert(\"agent.routing\".to_string(), source.clone());\n1735\t            }\n1736\t        }\n1737\t\n1738\t        if let Some(ref strands) =                config.strands.weave = weave_cfg;\n1742\t                }\n1743\t                sources.insert(\"strands.weave\".to_string(), source.clone());\n1744\t            }\n1745\t            if let Some(ref pulse_val) = strands.pulse {\n1746\t                if let Ok(pulse_cfg) = serde_yaml::from_value::<PulseConfig>(pulse_val.clone()) {\n1747\t                    config.strands.pulse = pulse_cfg;\n1748\t                }\n1749\t                sources.insert(\"strands.pulse\".to_string(), source.clone());\n1750\t            }\n1751\t            if let Some(ref unravel_val) = strands.unravel {\n1752\t                if let Ok(unravel_cfg) =\n1753\t                    serde_yaml::from_value::<UnravelConfig>(unravel_val.clone())\n1754\t                {\n1755\t                    config.strands.unravel = unravel_cfg;\n1756\t                }\n1757\t                sources.insert(\"strands.unravel\".to_string(), source.clone());\n1758\t            }\n1759\t        }\n1760\t\n1761\t        if let Some(ref prompt) = overrides.prompt {\n1762\t            config.prompt = prompt.clone();\n1763\t            sources.insert(\"prompt.context_files\".to_string(), source.clone());\n1764\t            sources.insert(\"prompt.instructions\".to_string(), source.clone());\n1765\t        }\n1766\t\n1767\t        if let Some(ref verification) = overrides.verification {\n1768\t            config.verification = verification.clone();\n1769\t            sources.insert(\"verification\".to_string(), source.clone());\n1770\t        }\n1771\t\n1772\t        if let if let Some(ref ws) = overrides.workspace {\n1778\t            if !ws.labels.is_empty() {\n1779\t                config.workspace.labels = ws.labels.clone();\n1780\t                sources.insert(\"workspace.labels\".to_string(), source);\n1781\t            }\n1782\t        }\n1783\t    }\n1784\t\n1785\t    /// Apply environment variable overrides (`NEEDLE_*` prefix, `__` separator).\n1786\t    ///\n1787\t    /// Example: `NEEDLE_AGENT__DEFAULT=opus` sets `agent.default` to `\"opus\"`.\n1788\t    pub fn apply_env_overrides(config: &mut Config, sources: &mut SourceMap) {\n1789\t        for (key, value) in std::env::vars() {\n1790\t            if let Some(suffix) = key.strip_prefix(\"NEEDLE_\") {\n1791\t                let config_path = }\n1931\t\n1932\t    /// Apply CLI overrides with source tracking.\n1933\t    pub fn apply_cli_overrides(\n1934\t        config: &mut Config,\n1935\t        overrides: CliOverrides,\n1936\t        sources: &mut SourceMap,\n1937\t    ) {\n1938\t        if let Some(ws) = overrides.workspace {\n1939\t            config.workspace.default = ws;\n1940\t            sources.insert(\"workspace.default\".to_string(), ConfigSource::CliOverride);\n1941\t        }\n1942\t        if let Some(agent) = overrides.agent_binary {\n1943\t            config.agent.default = agent;\n1944\t            sources.insert(\"agent.default\".to_string(), ConfigSource::CliOverride);\n1945\t        }\n1946\t        if let Some(n) = overrides.max_workers {\n1947\t            config.worker.max_workers = n;\n1948\t            sources.insert(\"worker.max_workers\".to_string(), ConfigSource::CliOverride);\n1949\t        }\n1950\t        // worker_name is handled at the Worker level, not stored in Config\n1951\t    }\n1952\t\n1953\t    /// Load the fully resolved configuration using the complete hierarchy:\n1954\t    ///\n1955\t    /// defaults → global file → workspace `.needle.yaml` → env vars → CLI args\n1956\t    ///\n1957\t    /// Returns the resolved config and a source map showing where each value\n1958\t    /// came from. The source map only contains entries for values that were\n1959\t    /// overridden from their defaults.\n1960\t    pub fn load_resolved(workspace_root: &Path, cli: CliOverrides) -> Result<(Config, SourceMap)> {\n1961\t        let mut sources = SourceMap::new();\n1962\t\n1963\t        // Layer 1 + 2: defaults + global config.\n1964\t        let global_path = dirs_or_home(\".config/needle/config.yaml\");\n1965\t        let mut config = Self::load_from_path(&global_path)?;\n1966\t\n1967\t        // Track which fields came from global config (if file existed).\n1968\t        if global_path.exists() {\n1969\t            let source = ConfigSource::GlobalFile(global_path);\n1970\t            // Mark all top-level sections as from global.\n1971\t            for key in &[\n1972\t                \"agent.default\",\n1973\t                \"agent.timeout\",\n1974\t                \"worker.max_workers\",\n1975\t                \"worker.idle_timeout\",\n1976\t                \"health.heartbeat_interval_secs\",\n1977\t                \"health.heartbeat_ttl_secs\",\n1978\t            ] {\n1979\t                sources.insert((*key).to_string(), source.clone());\n1980\t            }\n1981\t        }\n1982\t\n1983\t        // Layer 3: workspace config.\n1984\t        if let Some(ws_overrides) = Self::load_workspace(workspace_root)? {\n1985\t            Self::apply_workspace(&mut config, &ws_overrides, workspace_root, &mut sources);\n1986\t        }\n1987\t\n1988\t        // Layer 4: environment variables.\n1989\t        Self::apply_env_overrides(&mut config, &mut sources);\n1990\t\n1991\t        // Layer 5: CLI arguments.\n1992\t        Self::apply_cli_overrides(&mut config, cli, &mut sources);\n1993\t\n1994\t        Ok((config, sources))\n1995\t    }\n1996\t\n1997\t    /// Validate a resolved config.\n1998\t    ///\n1999\t    /// Returns a list of errors (empty = valid).\n2000\t    pub fn validate(config: &Config) -> Vec<ConfigError> {\n2001\t        let mut errors = Vec::new();\n2002\t\n2003\t        if config.agent.default.is_empty() {\n2004\t            errors.push(ConfigError {\n2005\t                field: \"agent.default\".to_string(),\n2006\t                message: \"must not be empty\".to_string(),\n2007\t            });\n2008\t        }\n2009\t\n2010\t        if config.worker.max_workers == 0 {\n2011\t            errors.push(ConfigError {\n2012\t                field: \"worker.max_workers\".to_string(),\n2013\t                message: \"must be at least 1\".to_string(),\n2014\t            });\n2015\t        }\n2016\t\n2017\t        if config.worker.max_workers > 50 {\n2018\t            errors.push(ConfigError {\n2019\t                field: \"worker.max_workers\".to_string(),\n2020\t                message: format!(\n2021\t                    \"{} exceeds practical fleet limit of 50\",\n2022\t                    config.worker.max_workers\n2023\t                ),\n2024\t            });\n2025\t        }\n2026\t\n2027\t        if config.worker.cpu_load_warn <= 0.0 || config.worker.cpu_load_warn > 1.0 {\n2028\t            errors.push(ConfigError {\n2029\t                field: \"worker.cpu_load_warn\".to_string(),\n2030\t                message: \"must be in range (0.0, 1.0]\".to_string(),\n2031\t            });\n2032\t        }\n2033\t\n2034\t        if config.health.heartbeat_ttl_secs < 3 * config.health.heartbeat_interval_secs {\n2035\t            errors.push(ConfigError {\n2036\t                field: \"health.heartbeat_ttl_secs\".to_string(),\n2037\t                message: format!(\n2038\t                    \"should be >= 3 * heartbeat_interval_secs ({}); detection may be unreliable\",\n2039\t                    3 * config.health.heartbeat_interval_secs\n2040\t                ),\n2041\t            });\n2042\t        }\n2043\t\n2044\t        // Validate routing regex patterns.\n2045\t        if let Some(ref routing) = config.agent.routing {\n2046\t            for (idx, rule) in routing.rules.iter().enumerate() {\n2047\t                // Validate that match_model is a valid regex.\n2048\t                if let Err(e) = regex::Regex::new(&rule.match_model) {\n2049\t                    errors.push(ConfigError {\n2050\t                        field: format!(\"agent.routing.rules[{}].match_model\", idx),\n2051\t                        message: format!(\"invalid regex pattern '{}': {}\", rule.match_model, e),\n2052\t                    });\n2053\t                }\n2054\t                // Validate that adapter is not empty.\n2055\t                if rule.adapter.is_empty() {\n2056\t                    errors.push(ConfigError {\n2057\t                        field: format!(\"agent.routing.rules[{}].adapter\", idx),\n2058\t                        message: \"must not be empty\".to_string(),\n2059\t                    });\n2060\t                }\n2061\t            }\n2062\t        }\n2063\t\n2064\t        errors\n2065\t    }\n2066\t\n2067\t    /// Format config values with source annotations for `--dump --show-source`.\n2068\t    pub fn dump_with_sources(config: &Config, sources: &SourceMap) -> Vec<String> {\n2069\t        let fields: Vec<(&str, String)> = vec![\n2070\t            (\"agent.default\", }\n2112\t}\n2113\t\n2114\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n2115\t// Helpers\n2116\t//
